## Review

**5 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent branded template, readable titles and a clear three-division structure, so the core story of what MedFire does and what it offers investors can be recovered. But slide 1 is an unformatted page with only 'Exhibit A Pitch Deck' in small text, the real title slide sits at slide 2, and slide 7 spends a full page on the logo. The deck repeats a who/what/how/revenue/statistics pattern three times with generic stock imagery (the arrow-on-chart picture, a stock chart for medical supplies, cash photos on revenue slides), and the leadership section is thin, with slides 10 and 11 showing names and photos with no credentials. The ask on slides 35 and 36 is stated plainly, but slide 16 shows a clipped footer, glossy 3D ovals with inconsistent rim colors, and no sense of a sequence, and the deck never shows any traction or results to support the pitch.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent header, logo and page-number template, the bold red and blue titles, and the large, scannable investment figures on slide 35.

**Most useful repair:** Remove or reformat slide 1, cut the logo-only slide 7, and fix the footer collision on slide 16 and the duplicated footer text.

**What would change the score:** I would compress the three repeated division sequences into one slide per division with a single message, replace the generic stock photos with product images or real data visuals, and give the team slides roles and credentials.

_Confidence high. 37 of 37 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide labeled Exhibit A Pitch Deck, followed by a legal disclaimer and a company origin story. It then moves through the company's culture of innovation, its three divisions (MedFire Innovations, MedFire Jobs and MedFire Expo), the company's assets and intellectual property, and its leadership team and advisors. The middle sections describe who each division serves, what it does, how it operates and how it earns revenue, supported by industry market-size statistics from IBISWorld. Near the end, the deck turns to a crowdfunding offer of 100,000 shares of Series A Preferred Stock at $10 per share, with a $1,000,000 total offering, a $500 minimum investment and a 6 percent cumulative yearly dividend. It closes with contact details and the company's San Diego address. Slides worth studying include the three-division layout on slide 6, the process diagram on slide 16 and the investment terms on slides 35 and 36.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the MedFire Innovations Inc. pitch deck have?**

The deck has 37 slides. It opens with a title slide labeled Exhibit A Pitch Deck and closes on slide 37 with the company's contact details and San Diego address.

**What investment terms does MedFire Innovations Inc. describe in its deck?**

On slide 35, MedFire Innovations, Inc. is offering 100,000 shares of Series A Preferred Stock at $10 per share, for a total offering of $1,000,000, with a minimum investment of $500. On slide 36, the deck states that the Preferred Stock will be entitled to receive cumulative dividends at a rate of 6 percent per year.

**What market size does the MedFire Innovations Inc. deck give for US medical device manufacturing?**

On slide 19, the deck lists Medical Device Manufacturing in the United States at $49.4bn, with market size in 2021 of 6.3% and market size growth in 2021 of 4.8%. The slide cites IBISWorld, December 8, 2020, for these industry statistics.

**How is the MedFire Innovations Inc. deck structured into divisions?**

Slide 6 states that MedFire has developed three distinct divisions: MedFire Innovations, MedFire Jobs and MedFire Expo. Slide 12 introduces the same three companies under the heading The MedFire Companies.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Born From a Single Idea…
MedFire Innovations Inc. was born with a single idea, and it was through the creation of our first product, we found the true value in developing key relationships and strategic resources. On June 22, 2016, MedFire Innovations, was formed as a corporation in the State of California. Since this time, MedFire has developed, manufactured, and brought to market our own innovative ideas, in the form of additional products and services. Realizing the value of our key relationships and resources, we expanded our services to include helping others create products through our custom collaborative process.
Our company is rapidly building assets and intellectual property for ourselves and for our clients. Since our inception, MedFire, an FDA Registered company, has obtained ten (10) United States Patents and two (2) Registered Trademarks. Additionally, we have multiple products in development, and multiple patents pending.
Moving forward, we envision a future where those with great ideas turn to us to develop these ideas into realities. MedFire is where Doctors, Nurses, Scientists, Public Safety Professionals, Veterinarians, and the like, come to move their ideas from the drafting table to the operating table, and from scratch paper to the field. A future that is only limited to one’s imagination. This is MedFire Innovations.
3
Copyright 2023 MedFire Innovations, Inc All Rights Reserved

### Slide 5

**Text**

Creating a Culture of Innovation
MedFire has developed a network of creative and innovative experts to help your ideas become reality. We have created a Culture of Innovation by sharing ideas and concepts within this network of biomedical, mechanical, and electrical engineers, to manufacturers, packaging experts, technical and regulatory specialists, and finally sales and marketing professionals, all with one goal in mind – To Bring Innovation To Life.
MedFire Innovations helps transform ideas into safe, profitable, and innovative medical products through a rapid and systematic design process. We can provide a broad range of services to support medical device development, which includes Class I, Class II, and Class III devices. From design, to engineering and integration, to testing and transfer, we will work with you at any phase of the design process. Whether you are seeking to improve an existing device or to develop a completely new medical device, we are prepared for the challenge.
4
Copyright 2023 MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title across the top left in two colours with the MedFire Innovations logo at the top right. Full-width justified paragraph below the title. Photo at lower left (about 5% to 46% wide) overlapped by a blue-outlined box that holds a black text panel on the right. Page number badge in the bottom right corner and a copyright footer centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Photograph at lower left: a gray-haired bearded man in a dark sweater points at a screen while a blonde woman in a white shirt with a lanyard leans over a clear-cased circuit board prototype on a lab bench. Another person with glasses is partly visible behind. Cool blue-teal lab lighting, with a ring lamp and monitors in the background. Boxed rectangle, cropped, with no tint. A blue (#103595) rectangle outline of about 3px surrounds the right text area and overlaps the photo's right edge. A black rectangle with a soft shadow sits inside it behind the white text. The MedFire Innovations logo is a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, the blue and red 'MedFire' wordmark with a red flame, and spaced blue 'INNOVATIONS' below it. Large faint pink-red curved arcs sit in the background at the top right and bottom left. A dashed red horizontal line and a dotted blue vertical line form a crosshair at the top left. A circular badge outlined in red and blue swooshes holds the page number at the bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid light gray #F5F5F5 with faint pink-red curved arcs at the top right and bottom left. Light gray #F5F5F5 background with translucent red #B5121B arcs at 14% opacity. Title in bold Arial sans-serif, red #B5121B for 'Creating a Culture' and blue #003794 for 'of Innovation', about 8% of the slide height. Body text in Arial regular, dark #181818, justified, about 3% of the slide height. 'MedFire' is bold red. 'To Bring Innovation To Life' is bold italic blue #103595. The panel text is white Arial. The footer is small black Arial on a #EEEEEE strip. The page number is bold blue. No gradients. The black panel has a drop shadow.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses three evenly weighted columns with a single headline to show the company's three divisions side by side, making the structure easy to scan.

**Text**

Serving Those Who Protect Us
MedFire has developed three distinct divisions to provide an array of services to those individuals and organization / dedicated to protecting those in need. Providing services to help develop products, devices, and services. Providing a / dedicated platform for employers and job seeks to connect. Providing a Marketplace for Exponential Brand Growth. / MedFire Helps Those Who Are In The Business  of Protecting Others.
Careers
Innovations
Marketplace
MedFire Jobs
MedFire Innovations
MedFire Expo
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All  Rights Reserved
5

**Layout**

Title across the top left with the MedFire Innovations logo at top right. A full-width white bordered text box sits below the title, then three equally spaced red down arrows in a row, each with a product logo underneath. Footer copyright is centred at the bottom and a round page-number badge sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Top-right MedFire Innovations logo: a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, with 'MedFire' in blue with a red flame over the 'i' and 'INNOVATIONS' spaced beneath. Three large red 3D bevelled down-pointing block arrows with blue outlines and a lighter red gradient on the right edge. Under them are logos: MedFire Jobs (circle emblem, blue 'MedFire' with red flame, outlined 'Jobs'), MedFire Innovations (emblem with stacked name and 'INNOVATIONS'), and MedFire Expo (emblem, blue 'MedFire', outlined 'Expo'). Pale translucent red curved swooshes in the top-right and bottom-left corners. A blue dashed vertical line and a red dashed horizontal line sit at the top-left near the title. A circular red and blue ring badge holds the page number at the bottom right. The text box has a thin blue border and a soft drop shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with translucent red curved swooshes at top-right and bottom-left. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with translucent red swooshes (#B5121B at 14% opacity). The title is bold Arial sans-serif, with 'Serving Those' in red #B5121B and 'Who Protect Us' in blue #103595, about 7.9% of slide height. Body text in the box is Arial regular #181818 and justified, about 3% of slide height, with 'MedFire' in bold red and the closing sentence in bold blue #103595. Arrow labels are bold white Arial at about 4.5-5% of slide height. The arrows are red #C0181F with blue #103595 outlines and a bevel. The footer is Arial 2% of slide height in black on a light grey #EEEEEE strip. The page number is bold blue.

**Text**

MedFire Innovations Logo
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved
6

**Layout**

Title at top left in a single line, starting about 5% from the left edge. A small MedFire logo sits in the top right corner. A very large MedFire Innovations logo is centred in the middle of the slide, spanning about 90% of the width. A centred copyright strip runs along the bottom and a circular page number badge sits in the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Large MedFire Innovations logo, centred: on the left a red and blue oval badge with a white ring inside and a white heartbeat (ECG) line with a dot at the bottom. The upper part of the badge is red and the lower part is blue, with the ECG line wavering across the boundary. To the right is the wordmark 'MedFire' in heavy sans-serif, with 'Med' in blue (#103595) and 'Fire' in red (#B5121B), and a red flame over the 'i'. Under the wordmark is 'INNOVATIONS' in widely letter-spaced blue capitals with a small TM mark. A smaller copy of the same logo is at the top right. Two pale pink (#B5121B at 14% opacity) curved arcs: one sweeping from the top right corner down the right side, one from the lower left corner. A thin dashed corner guide at top left: a dashed blue vertical line at about 4% from the left and a dashed red horizontal line below the title. The bottom right page-number badge is a circle outlined in a red and blue swirl.

**Design**

Background: Solid #F5F5F5 light grey with translucent pink (#B5121B, 14% opacity) curved shapes at the top right and bottom left. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with soft translucent pink arcs. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif, about 7.9% of slide height, with 'MedFire Innovations' in red #B5121B and 'Logo' in blue #103595. Footer text in regular Arial, about 2% of slide height, black on a light grey #EEEEEE strip. Page number bold blue #103595. Brand colours are red #B5121B and blue #103595. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Central circular DNA image ringed by a grey arc, with eight lightbulb icon circles arranged around it (four left, four right) connected by thin grey leader lines to small ring markers on the arc. Left labels are right-aligned, right labels left-aligned. Footer copyright box bottom-centre, circular page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Central circular image of a blue-tinted DNA double helix against a dark teal background, boxed in a thick cobalt-blue glossy ring. Around it is a light grey circular ring split into gaps at the top and bottom. Eight glossy round buttons with white lightbulb icons: red top-left (Multiple divisions), red top-right (Incorporated), blue mid-left (Multiple ideas), blue mid-right (FDA), red lower-left (Multiple products), red lower-right (Holder of 10), blue bottom-left (Trademarks), blue bottom-right (Patents pending). Thin grey leader lines run from each button to small ring markers on the grey ring, red-outlined markers for red buttons and blue-outlined for blue buttons, with white fill. MedFire Innovations logo top-right: red circle with a heartbeat line and blue swoosh, blue 'MedFire' wordmark with a red flame, and 'INNOVATIONS' in spaced blue letters. Pale pink translucent curved bands in the bottom-left and top-right corners. Red dashed horizontal line and blue dotted vertical line at top-left form a crosshair. Page number sits inside a circle with a red and blue swirl outline at the bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with pale pink translucent curved bands in the bottom-left and top-right corners. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with pale pink translucent arcs (#B5121B at 14% opacity). Title 'Company' in red #B5121B and 'DNA' in navy blue #103595, bold Arial sans-serif, about 8% of slide height. Body labels in regular Arial, dark grey #181818, about 3.5% of slide height. Red and blue glossy buttons with gradient shading. Footer in small Arial black on a light grey #EEEEEE box. Page number bold navy #103595.

### Slide 9

**Text**

The MedFire Team
MedFire Innovations, Incorporated is led by our Chairman and Chief Executive Officer, Gregory Christmas. Mr. Christmas has put together a team of individuals and groups that provide all the experience and skills necessary to ensure the success of MedFire Innovations and all its divisions. From Doctors and Nurses, to Scientists and Bioengineers, EMT’s and Paramedics, Firefighters, Engineers, Machinists, Fabricators, IT Professionals, IP Professionals, Marketing Experts, and more. Our combined team is seasoned in their expertise and ready for a challenge.

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a dashed crosshair guide; MedFire Innovations logo at top right. A large photo is on the right half (from about x 54% to the right edge) in the middle of the slide. A black translucent text box with a red outline overlaps the left part of the photo and spans the left two thirds. Round page number badge bottom right, copyright footer centered at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the right (about 46% wide, 55% tall, full to the right edge): top-down close-up of several people's hands stacked together in a huddle, with sleeves in pink knit, orange, dark green knit, blue knit, cream and a light blue scrunchie on a wrist. Warm, muted lighting, with the hands in the center and the picture cropped tightly. The left edge of the photo is covered by the text box. A red rectangle outline (about 2px, #B5121B, no fill) frames the text box and is offset slightly up and to the left of it. A dark charcoal translucent box with a soft drop shadow holds the white text. Top-left has a thin dashed red horizontal line and a dashed blue vertical line forming a crosshair. Pale pink translucent curved arcs sit in the top right and bottom left corners. The MedFire Innovations logo is at top right: a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line and a flame over blue and red 'MedFire' lettering with spaced 'INNOVATIONS' beneath. Bottom right is a round badge with a blue and red swirl ring around the number 8. Bottom center is a light grey tab behind the copyright line.

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with translucent pink arcs in the top right and bottom left corners. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with pale pink arc shapes (#B5121B at 14% opacity). ### Slide 13

**Text**

MedFire Innovations
MedFire Innovations is our primary division, as well as the name of our Company. This division focuses on the development of our Company products, devices, and services. This division provides services to outside individuals and entities seeking various services our company provides for the development of original ideas for the medical, science, and public safety industries. Some of these services include assistance with the design and development of a device, feasibility studies, concept development, design for manufacturing, small batch prototype production, verification and validation, FDA clearance and approval, intellectual property and branding, marketing and sales, and distribution and fulfillment.
12
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title at top left in large bold type, with the MedFire Innovations logo at top right. A large scroll-shaped outlined box spans most of the slide width in the middle, holding one justified paragraph with generous margins. A centered copyright footer sits at the bottom and a circular page-number badge sits at the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Red (#B5121B) outlined scroll banner with rolled curls at the top right and the bottom left; the left curl extends down as a tall rounded bar to about 83% of the slide height. The outline is about 0.4% of the slide width and the fill is transparent. Pale pink translucent curved bands (red at 14% opacity) sweep across the bottom-left corner and the top-right corner. A thin dashed red horizontal line and a thin dotted blue vertical line cross at the top left near the title, like a crosshair. The MedFire Innovations logo at top right has a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, the 'MedFire' wordmark in blue and red with a small red flame, and spaced letters 'INNOVATIONS' underneath. The page number sits in a circular badge with a blue and red swirling outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with translucent pink curved bands (#B5121B at 14% opacity) at the bottom left and top right. Light grey background (#F5F5F5). The title is bold Arial sans-serif, with 'MedFire' in red #B5121B and 'Innovations' in blue #103595, about 7.9% of the slide height. The body is Arial regular, dark grey #181818, about 3.5% of the slide height and justified, with the bold red lead-in 'MedFire Innovations'. The footer is Arial, about 2% of the slide height, black on a light grey #EEEEEE strip. The page number is bold blue #103595. Accents are red and blue. There are no shadows or gradients, and the scroll has rounded corners.

### Slide 14

**Text**

**Layout**

Title at top-left with a dashed crosshair guide; MedFire logo at top-right. A segmented photo of two hikers sits on the left half, a dark grey semicircular arc in the centre, and four icon-plus-label rows stacked on the right. Copyright footer is centred at the bottom and a circular page badge sits at the bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left: a photo of two silhouetted hikers on a rocky ridge at sunset, one reaching a hand down to help the other up. It is sliced into six vertical rounded pill shapes with thin gold outlines and soft drop shadows. The pills alternate in height, tall then short. Sky is teal fading to orange and yellow at the horizon, and the rocks are near-black navy. Centre: a thick dark grey half-circle arc, open to the left, with white round end caps. Four small ring nodes sit on it, alternating red, blue, red, blue from top to bottom. Thin grey connector lines run from the nodes to four glossy round icons: a red doctor with a stethoscope (top), a blue circle with a white plus sign, a red stethoscope, and a blue lightbulb with a gear. Top right: MedFire Innovations logo, a red and blue circular heartbeat emblem with a red flame over the wordmark 'MedFire' in blue and red and 'INNOVATIONS' in spaced gold-brown capitals. Pale pink curved bands in the top-right and bottom-left corners. Bottom right: a circular badge outlined in blue and red swoosh containing the page number. Dashed red horizontal line and dotted blue vertical line at the top-left form a corner guide.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with pale pink translucent curved bands at top-right and bottom-left. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with translucent pink (#B5121B at 14% opacity) arcs in the corners. Title 'WHO' in bold red #B5121B and 'we help' in bold navy #103595, Arial Bold, about 8% of slide height. Body labels are Arial regular in near-black #181818, about 3.5% of slide height. Icons are glossy radial-gradient circles in red and navy blue. Photo pills have gold outlines and drop shadows. Footer is Arial 2% in black on a light grey #EEEEEE strip.

### Slide 15

**Text**

WHAT we do
MedFire Innovations, Incorporated was founded because one person wanted to help those who help others, and it is on this statement “Protecting Those Who Are In The Business Of Protecting Others” our company stands. Since our beginning, we have made it our mission to help bring to market the ideas that will help make our world a better and safer place. To develop ideas that protect patients and patient care providers, and those working on the front lines of public safety. We can do this because we have assembled a team of partners that are dedicated to helping make ideas become reality.
We help make Ideas become reality by using a user-oriented industrial design process based on the Design Thinking methodology. We provide a broad range of services to support medical device development, including Class I, Class II, and Class III devices - to devices used by those working on the frontlines in public safety. From initial concept, to design, to engineering and integration, to testing and transfer, we will work with you at any phase of the design process. Whether you are seeking to improve an existing device, or design and develop a completely new and innovative device, we are prepared for the challenge.
14
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title at top left with a dashed crosshair guide at its lower left; MedFire Innovations logo at top right. Two equal white text boxes side by side in the lower two-thirds, each topped by a round icon straddling its top edge. Page number in a circle at bottom right; centred copyright strip at bottom.

**Visuals**

Logo top right: MedFire Innovations, a round red and blue emblem with a white heartbeat/pulse line, 'MedFire' in blue with red 'Fire' and a red flame above, and 'INNOVATIONS' in spaced blue capitals below. Red glossy circular icon with a white lightbulb outline above the left box. Blue glossy circular icon with a white handshake above the right box. Two white boxes with thin grey borders and soft drop shadows. Dashed red horizontal line and dotted blue vertical line at top left forming a crosshair. Pale translucent red curved swooshes at the left bottom and right edge. Page-number badge at bottom right: a circle outlined with a red-and-blue swirl ring.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with pale translucent red curved swoosh shapes at bottom left and right edge. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with translucent red (#B5121B at 14%) curved swooshes. Title 'WHAT' in bold red #B5121B and 'we do' in bold blue #103595, Arial Bold sans-serif, about 8% of slide height. Body text Arial regular, dark #181818, about 3.2% of slide height, justified, with a red bold company name and blue bold-italic quote. Boxes are white with grey outlines and soft shadows. Page number bold blue. Footer is small black Arial on a light grey strip #EEEEEE.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Arranges twelve numbered process steps around a central 'How we do it' label, turning a long service list into a visual sequence.
- Review: The ask on slides 35 and 36 is stated plainly, but slide 16 shows a clipped footer, glossy 3D ovals with inconsistent rim colors, and no sense of a sequence, and the deck never shows any traction or results to support the pitch.
- Suggested repair: Remove or reformat slide 1, cut the logo-only slide 7, and fix the footer collision on slide 16 and the duplicated footer text.

**Layout**

Hub-and-spoke diagram: a large central blue circle with twelve glossy oval number badges arranged around it in a ring, each joined by a dashed grey spoke. Labels sit outside the ovals, left-side labels right-aligned and right-side labels left-aligned, with Concept Development above and Industrial Design below. Title top-left, logo top-right, page number in a circle at bottom-right, copyright footer centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Central 3D glossy navy-blue disc with a red rim and an inner lighter blue circle holding the text HOW we do it. Twelve glossy 3D oval badges with a white bold number each: 1 and 7 red with brown/bronze rims; 2 blue with red rim; 3 and 9 red with gold/orange rims; 4 and 10 blue with black rims; 5 and 11 red with yellow-green rims; 6 blue with teal rim; 8 blue with dark red rim; 12 blue with teal rim. Grey square-dotted dashed spokes connect each oval to the central disc. MedFire Innovations logo at top-right: a red circle with a white heartbeat/ECG line and a blue swoosh, and blue 'MedFire' wordmark with a red flame above 'i' and spaced letters 'INNOVATIONS' beneath. A thin dotted blue vertical line and red dashed horizontal line form a crosshair at top-left. Translucent pink-red curved swooshes at bottom-left and right edge. Page number sits in a white circle with a blue and red swirling outline at bottom-right. A small light-grey footer tab holds the copyright text.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with translucent pink-red curved swooshes at bottom-left and right. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with pale red (#B5121B at 14% opacity) curved swooshes at bottom-left and right edge. Title in Arial Bold: 'HOW' red #B5121B and 'we do it' blue #103595, about 8% of slide height. Labels in Arial Bold, dark #181818, about 3% height. Numbers white bold about 6.9% height. Glossy 3D bevelled ovals with gradients and highlights. Footer text small grey and black.

**Layout**

Title at top left with a dashed crosshair guide; MedFire logo at top right. A circular photo with red and blue crescent swooshes sits left of centre. A white card with a red headline sits upper right, and a smaller white card with blue justified body text sits below it at lower right. Page number in a circle at bottom right; copyright footer centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Circular photo, about 25% of slide width, of a person in a dark navy suit touching a glowing blue hexagon labelled INNOVATION on a translucent hexagon grid (other cells: Idea, Research, Improvement, Concept). Cool blue-grey tones, soft focus, blurred background. A red tapered swoosh arcs over the top-left of the circle, and a thick blue crescent arc hugs its right edge. MedFire Innovations logo at top right: red circle with a heartbeat/pulse line and blue lower arc, 'MedFire' in blue and red bold letters with a red flame over the 'i', and 'INNOVATIONS' in spaced blue capitals below. Large pale pink arcs at the top right and bottom left corners (red at 14% opacity). Thin dashed crosshair guide at top left: a vertical blue dotted line at x≈4% and a horizontal red dashed line at y≈11%. A red and blue circular ring around the page number at bottom right. Both white cards have soft grey drop shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with large pale pink arc shapes in the top-right and bottom-left corners. Light grey background #F5F5F5. Titles and headline in bold red #B5121B, with 'Businesses' in bold navy blue #103595. Body text in regular navy #103595. Arial / Helvetica sans-serif throughout. Title about 7.9% of slide height, card headline about 5.9%, body about 3.9%. White cards #FFFFFF with subtle shadows and square corners. The footer sits on a light grey #EEEEEE strip with black text.

**Layout**

Title at top left with the MedFire logo at top right. A central circular hub with a lightbulb sits in the middle, with eight product circles arranged around it in a ring (two top, two bottom, two left, two right). Each circle has a bold label beside it: left labels right-aligned to the left of the circles, right labels left-aligned. Footer copyright strip is centered at the bottom, and the page number sits in a circle at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Central hub: a thick glossy blue ring (about 15% wide) enclosing a red outline lightbulb icon. Around it is a light grey ring made of two arc segments with a gap at the top and bottom. Eight small circle nodes sit on the ring: red-outlined at top-left, top-right, mid-left lower and mid-right lower; blue-outlined at left upper, right upper, bottom-left and bottom-right. Thin grey connector lines link each node to a product circle. Eight glossy 3D circular buttons with product images on white or transparent backgrounds. Red buttons: Path-O-Cap (a green-covered hospital bed labelled CLEAN), Dental Instrument (multicoloured dental tools in a white square), Sere Tool (black-and-white tool set on white), Cell Delivery System (white handheld device with a nozzle). Blue buttons: The Recopad (a white diaper with tubing), Self-Pap (a salmon-pink handle with a dark stem), Williams Key (black L-shaped keys on white), Path-O-Wrap (a stretcher with three camouflage swatches). The MedFire Innovations logo at top right has a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, red 'Med' and blue 'Fire' wordmark with a flame, and spaced blue 'INNOVATIONS'. Large pale red curved swooshes in the top right and bottom left corners. A blue dotted vertical line and a red dashed horizontal line form a corner crosshair at top left. A circular outlined badge at bottom right holds the page number. A light grey strip sits behind the copyright text.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with translucent red curved swooshes at top right and bottom left. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with translucent red swooshes (#B5121B at 14% opacity). The title 'MedFire' is red #B5121B and 'Innovations' is blue #103595, in bold Arial sans-serif at about 7.9% of the slide height. 'and many more..' is dark #181818 bold Arial at about 5% of the height. Labels are bold Arial in #181818 at about 3% of the height. Red/blue glossy buttons use gradients and a bevelled 3D look. The grey ring is #BFBFBF. The page number is bold blue #103595. The footer is regular Arial in black at about 2% of the height on a #EEEEEE strip.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Revenue
MedFire Innovations will generate revenues by:
Selling products, devices, and services developed and manufactured by MedFire Innovations.
Selling products, devices, and services designed, developed, by MedFire Innovations for contracted customers.
Providing various services including; consulting, concept and design, and development services to individuals and groups interested in product and device development for new - innovative products and devices.
Product Licensing & Royalties
20
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title 'Revenue' top-left with a dashed crosshair guide; the MedFire Innovations logo sits top-right. A rotated-square photo frame with blue and red chevron borders fills the left half. The right half holds a blue heading and four icon-plus-text rows stacked vertically with left-aligned text. A footer copyright strip is centred at the bottom and a round page-number badge sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left: a diamond-rotated photograph of a hand holding a fanned stack of US $100 bills, shallow depth of field, warm brown and grey tones, with a white border. Behind it is a blue L-shaped chevron band at the upper left and a red L-shaped chevron band at the lower right. Four identical round red icons with a dark outline and a glossy look, each showing a hand holding green banknotes under a gold dollar coin, in a column to the left of the text rows. Top-right: the MedFire Innovations logo, a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, the blue 'MedFire' wordmark with a red flame over the i, and the spaced letters 'INNOVATIONS' below. Faint pink translucent curved shapes sit in the bottom-left and top-right corners. A thin red dashed horizontal line and a blue dotted vertical line form a crosshair next to the title. The bottom-right page number sits inside a white circle with a thin blue and red arc outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with translucent pink (#B5121B at 14% opacity) curved shapes in the bottom-left and top-right corners. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with soft pink arcs in the corners. Title is bold red #B5121B Arial. Heading is bold blue #103595 Arial. Body text is regular Arial in dark grey #181818. Accents are blue #103595 and red #B5121B. Title is about 8% of the slide height, heading about 4.4%, body about 3.2%. The footer is small black Arial on a light grey #EEEEEE strip. Flat shapes, no shadows except the glossy icons.

### Slide 22

**Text**

MedFire Jobs
“Where Heroes Come To Get Hired”
MedFire Jobs is a “niche” job search platform, focused on jobs and careers for those who currently work, or are seeking employment in the fields of Medicine, Science, Nursing, Fire and EMS, Law Enforcement, Veterinary Medicine, and other Public Safety professions. Its features include a hiring platform to assist employers with recruiting, from detailed job posting services to video interview capabilities to meet and assess applicable candidates. The platform further enables Featured Company and Featured Advertisement services that allow employers to improve visibility of their job and career offerings, and more. This tailored platform provides one-of-a-kind user experience for employers and job seekers within this industry sector.
21
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a dotted crosshair guide; MedFire Innovations logo at top right. A centred group photo sits below the title, then the MedFire Jobs logo, a centred red tagline banner, and a wide white bordered text box spanning nearly the full width with justified text. Circular page number at bottom right and a copyright footer centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Photo of five professionals in a row, cut out from their background, shoulder-up to waist crops: a man in a dark navy uniform smiling, a woman with auburn hair in a white coat holding a small tan dog and a dark cat, a man in a white lab coat with stethoscope and blue tie with arms crossed, a woman in blue scrubs with stethoscope and arms crossed, and a firefighter in a black helmet and tan turnout gear with yellow reflective stripes. Lighting is even studio lighting. The bottom edge of the photo is cropped flat. MedFire Innovations logo top right: a red circle with a white heartbeat/ECG line and blue swoosh, blue 'MedFire' wordmark with a red flame over the i, and spaced letters 'INNOVATIONS' below. MedFire Jobs logo: the same circular emblem followed by 'MedFire' in blue and red with a flame and 'Jobs' in outlined blue lettering, with a small TM. A dashed red-blue crosshair guide at top left: a vertical dotted blue line at x about 4% and a horizontal dashed red line at y about 11%. Two large pale pink curved arcs at the top right and bottom left corners. A circular blue and red ring around the page number at bottom right. The white text box has a thin blue border and a subtle drop shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with pale red translucent arcs at the top right and bottom left. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with translucent pale red arcs (#B5121B at 14% opacity) in the corners. Title in bold Arial sans-serif, 'MedFire' red #B5121B and 'Jobs' blue #103595, about 8% of slide height. Tagline in bold white Arial on a red #B5121B rectangle, about 4% of height. Body text in regular Arial #181818, about 3% of height, justified, with 'MedFire Jobs' in bold red. White box #FFFFFF with a blue border and soft shadow. Page number bold blue #103595. Footer in small Arial on a light grey #EEEEEE strip.

**Layout**

Title top-left with the MedFire Jobs logo beneath it and the MedFire Innovations logo top-right. An arched photo sits at the left-middle with an arrow pointing right to two columns of six bulleted items each, linked by thin vertical connector lines. Page number is bottom-right in a circle and a copyright footer is bottom-centre.

**Visuals**

Arched-top photograph of two hands reaching toward each other over a grey-green rock face, against a blue-grey mountain sky. It has a thin blue outline and a soft shadow. A dark grey outlined arch is offset to the left and overlaps it. A thin dark grey horizontal arrow runs from the photo's upper right to the list, pointing right. Twelve circular bullet markers in two columns: each has a red outer ring and white gap, with a blue or red filled centre. Colours alternate down each column: blue, red, blue, red, blue, red. Thin vertical connector lines between markers alternate red and blue. The MedFire Innovations logo is a red and blue circular heartbeat emblem with blue 'MedFire' wordmark, a red flame and 'INNOVATIONS' in letter-spaced text. The MedFire Jobs logo has the same emblem with 'MedFire' in blue and 'Jobs' in light blue outline. A dashed red horizontal line and a dotted blue vertical line cross at the top-left corner. Two pale pink curved bands sit at the top-right and bottom-left. A circle with red and blue swoosh outline holds the page number at bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid #F5F5F5 with pale red translucent curved bands at top-right and bottom-left. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with pale translucent red swooshes (#B5121B at 14% opacity). Title 'WHO' in red #B5121B and 'we help' in blue #103595, Arial Bold, about 8% of slide height. List text is dark #181818 Arial regular, about 3.5% of slide height. The footer is Arial about 2% in black on a light grey #EEEEEE strip. The page number is bold blue #103595. Thin strokes, no gradients, soft shadow on the photo.

**Layout**

Title at top left with a dotted guide-line crosshair at its left; MedFire Innovations logo at top right; MedFireJobs logo below the title at left. A half-ring (semicircle) diagram of five wedge segments is centred in the lower half, with a label beside each segment: centre label above, two on each side. Page number in a circle at bottom right; copyright strip centred at bottom.

**Visuals**

Semicircular segmented diagram in 3 concentric rings and 5 radial sectors, divided by thin light gaps. Outer ring: left dark blue (#1C5179), upper-left light teal-blue (#8BC5D2), top orange (#ED7249), upper-right light teal-blue (#8BC5D0), right dark blue (#19537C). Inner ring: left darkest navy (#103650), upper-left teal (#3A808E), top red-orange (#D14215), upper-right teal (#3A808E), right navy (#103650). The innermost space is empty off-white and holds a large cartoon icon of a call-centre/support person with a headset, blue shirt, dark hair, and an orange gear with yellow centre and a grey wrench. Five smaller circular badges with a light grey ring sit on the outer ring edge, each containing the same support-person icon: bottom-left on a dark navy circle, upper-left on a teal circle, top on an orange-red circle, upper-right on a teal circle, bottom-right on a dark navy circle. Logos: MedFire Innovations logo (red and blue circular emblem with heartbeat line, blue 'MedFire' wordmark with red F and flame, letter-spaced 'INNOVATIONS') at top right; MedFireJobs logo (circular emblem, blue and red wordmark with outlined 'Jobs') at left. Large pale pink (red at 14% opacity) curved swooshes at bottom-left and top-right corners. Dotted guide lines: a red dashed horizontal line and a blue dotted vertical line at top left. A circular outline ring in red and blue around the page number at bottom right. Light grey strip behind the copyright text.

**Layout**

Title 'Revenue' at top left beside a dotted crosshair guide. Left half holds a framed photo over nested red and blue diamonds; right half holds a blue heading and three icon-plus-text rows stacked vertically. MedFire Innovations logo top right, MedFire Jobs logo bottom left, copyright strip bottom centre, circular page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Photo of a small house folded from US $100 bills on a white background, soft shadow, shown in a square-ish frame with a dark grey border (about 1% thick), overlapping the diamonds. Behind it: a large red diamond outline (chevron-like corner) and two blue diamonds with a thin white diamond outline between them. Three identical round icons: a glossy dark-blue circle with a hand holding green banknotes and a gold dollar coin. A thin dashed red horizontal line and a dotted blue vertical line near the top-left form a crosshair. Large pale pink arcs sweep the top-right and bottom-left corners. MedFire Innovations logo: red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, blue 'MedFire' with red flame over the i, and spaced letters 'INNOVATIONS' in blue below. MedFire Jobs logo: the same emblem, with blue 'MedFire' and light-blue outline 'Jobs', a red flame. Page number sits in a white circle with a red and blue swirl outline.

**Design**

Background: Solid #F5F5F5 with pale pink (#B5121B at 14% opacity) arcs in the top-right and bottom-left corners. Light grey background #F5F5F5 with pale pink arcs (#B5121B at 14% opacity). Title in bold red #B5121B Arial, about 7.9% of slide height. Heading in bold blue #103595 Arial, about 4.4%. Body text in regular Arial, dark grey #181818, about 3.5%. Accents in red #B5121B and blue #103595. Flat shapes with no gradients. The photo frame has a subtle drop shadow. The copyright strip is a light grey #EEEEEE box with black text at about 2%.

### Slide 28

**Text**

Marketplace
“A Marketplace For Exponential Brand Growth”
MedFire Expo is a unique online marketplace environment to foster and nurture key business
relationships. The Expo will provide organizations with a place to connect sales teams with sales
prospects, identify new markets, and grow brand through strategic alignment and networking.
Those who use the Expo will be provided a unique visual experience providing an enhanced
customer experience. Expo Members will enjoy a platform to shop for products and services that
cater to those who are in the business of serving others.
27
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title 'Marketplace' at top left beside a dotted crosshair guide. MedFire Innovations logo at top right. Centered MedFire Expo logo below the title. A wide rounded panel spans most of the slide width: a blue header band holds a boxed tagline, and a grey body holds a justified paragraph. A page number badge is at bottom right and a copyright footer sits bottom centre.

**Visuals**

MedFire Expo logo: a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, then 'MedFire' in blue and red and 'Expo' in white-filled outlined blue letters, with a small red flame above the 'i' and a TM mark. MedFire Innovations logo at top right: the same circular emblem with 'MedFire' in blue and red, a flame, and spaced blue 'INNOVATIONS' beneath. A pale pink swoosh arc crosses the top right and another sits at bottom left. A red dashed horizontal line and a blue dotted vertical line form a crosshair at top left. A page-number badge at bottom right is a circular swirl ring in red and blue around '27'. The rounded panel has a soft drop shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with pale pink translucent red arc shapes at top right and bottom left. Light grey #F5F5F5 background with translucent red #B5121B swooshes at 14% opacity. Title in bold red #B5121B Arial, about 8% of the slide height. Panel header is royal blue #103595 with a white double-line rectangular border around the tagline in bold white Arial, about 4% of the height. Panel body is light grey #D9D9D9 with large rounded corners at top left, top right, bottom left and bottom right, and a soft shadow. Body text is regular Arial in dark #181818, about 3.5% of the height, justified, with the 'MedFire Expo' words in bold red. The footer is small black Arial on a light grey #EEEEEE strip.

### Slide 30

**Text**

WHAT we do
Provide a Marketplace between / retailers, wholesalers, and / manufacturers, and the medical, / science, and public safety / community.
Provide a “niche” to those who are / targeting and selling to those / working in these professions.
Provide specialized B2B, and B2C / for specialized products such as; / medical devices and consumables
29
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a dotted crosshair guide; MedFire Innovations logo top right. The left column holds the MedFire Expo logo above a word-cloud handshake image. A central vertical arc links three circular handshake icons, each with an arrow pointing right to a text block in the right column. Page number sits in a circular emblem at bottom right and the copyright bar is centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

MedFire Innovations logo (red and blue circular emblem with heartbeat line, flame, blue 'MedFire' wordmark, 'INNOVATIONS' spaced beneath). MedFire Expo logo (same emblem, blue 'MedFire' and outlined 'Expo' wordmark, red flame). Word-cloud illustration of two clasped hands formed from words such as cooperate, communicate, unite, serve, collaborate, help out, assist, in tan/brown tones with dark brown outline shapes, no background. Three white circles with dark grey outline, each holding a red handshake icon, at x 38.6/y 24.8, x 51.3/y 47.7 and x 38.6/y 74.5. Dark grey horizontal lines run from the left edge to the top and bottom circles. A dark grey curved arc connects the three circles. Each circle has a dark grey dot followed by a dark grey horizontal arrow pointing right. Large pale red translucent arcs at the bottom left and top right corners. Dotted dark blue vertical line and dashed red horizontal line near top left forming a crosshair. Circular page-number emblem with red and blue swoosh ring at bottom right. **Visuals**

Semicircular fan diagram of five wedge segments, with inner arc bands. Left to right: dark blue outer wedge (#1C5179), light blue wedge (#8BC5D2), orange wedge (#ED7249) at top centre, light blue wedge (#8BC5D0), dark blue outer wedge (#19537C). The inner arcs are dark navy (#103650), teal (#3A808E) and burnt orange (#D14215) under the centre wedge. Each segment carries a circular icon with a grey ring: a headset-wearing support person with a blue shirt, an orange gear and a wrench. The icon backgrounds are dark navy, teal and orange. A rounded blue rectangle (#103595) in the centre holds 'By providing:' in white. Logos: MedFire Innovations (red/blue circular emblem with a flame and a heartbeat line, 'MedFire' in blue and red, 'INNOVATIONS' spaced) top-right, and MedFireExpo (same emblem, 'MedFire' with outlined 'Expo') at left. Faint pink curved swooshes at the bottom-left and top-right corners. A blue/red circular swirl ring surrounds the page number. A dashed red horizontal line and a dotted blue vertical line sit at the top-left.

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with faint pink (#B5121B at 14% opacity) curved swoosh shapes at the bottom-left and top-right. Light grey background (#F5F5F5). **Layout**

Title at top left in two colours, with the MedFire Innovations logo at top right. A left column holds an outlined headline box, a four-line paragraph and a wavy blue banner. A framed portrait photo fills the right-centre. Page number sits in a circle at bottom right and the copyright footer is centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

MedFire Innovations logo at top right: a red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, the wordmark 'MedFire' in blue with a red flame over the i, and 'INNOVATIONS' in spaced blue capitals beneath. Portrait photo of a smiling woman with long dark hair in a white lab coat over a blue collared shirt, arms crossed, on a white background. She is shown from the waist up, centred, with soft even lighting. The photo sits in a rounded dark-grey tablet-style frame with a faint mirror reflection below. A wavy-edged cyan banner has a soft drop shadow. A rounded rectangle with a cyan outline surrounds the headline. Pale red translucent arcs sweep across the bottom-left and top-right corners. A dotted red horizontal line and a dotted blue vertical line cross at the top left. A small circular swirl in red and blue rings the page number.

Background: Solid off-white #F5F5F5 with translucent pale red arcs in the bottom-left and top-right corners. Off-white background #F5F5F5. The body is Arial regular in near-black #181818 at about 4%, with 'MedFire Innovations' in bold red #C00000. The banner quote is bold italic Arial in black at about 4%. The accents are cyan #00B0F0 and translucent pale red #B5121B at 14% opacity. The headline box has a cyan stroke of about 0.4% and large rounded corners. The banner has a drop shadow. The footer sits on a light grey #EEEEEE strip.

### Slide 35

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: States the offering size, price per share and minimum investment in large, short lines, so the core terms can be read at a glance.
- Review: The ask on slides 35 and 36 is stated plainly, but slide 16 shows a clipped footer, glossy 3D ovals with inconsistent rim colors, and no sense of a sequence, and the deck never shows any traction or results to support the pitch.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent header, logo and page-number template, the bold red and blue titles, and the large, scannable investment figures on slide 35.

**Text**

The Investment
MedFire Innovations, Inc.
is
OFFERING 100,000 SHARES OF SERIES A
PREFERRED STOCK AT $10 PER SHARE
TOTAL OFFERING $1,000,000
Minimum Investment is $500
34
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title at top left with a dashed crosshair guide; MedFire logo at top right. A large rounded rectangle panel fills the middle, holding five centred lines of text. Page number in a circle at bottom right and a copyright footer centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

MedFire Innovations logo at top right: red and blue circular emblem with a heartbeat line, blue 'MedFire' wordmark with a red flame over the 'i', and spaced 'INNOVATIONS' below. Faint pink curved sweeping bands at the top right and bottom left. Thin dashed blue vertical line and dashed red horizontal line at top left forming crosshair guides. Large rounded-rectangle panel with thick blue outline, light grey fill and soft drop shadow. Small circular ring graphic in red and blue around the page number at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F5F5F5 with faint translucent red curved bands at the top right and bottom left. Light grey #F5F5F5 background with translucent red #B5121B swooshes at 14% opacity. Title in bold red #B5121B Arial, about 8% of slide height. Panel text in bold Arial: red #B5121B for key figures (about 8% height) and near-black #181818 for the rest (about 6% height). Panel fill #D9D9D9, outline blue #103595 about 0.5% thick, large corner radius about 10% of height, soft shadow. Page number bold blue #103595. Footer small Arial black on a pale #EEEEEE strip.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Review: The ask on slides 35 and 36 is stated plainly, but slide 16 shows a clipped footer, glossy 3D ovals with inconsistent rim colors, and no sense of a sequence, and the deck never shows any traction or results to support the pitch.

**Text**

Dividend
What do I receive for my investment?
A 6% Yearly Dividend
Investor participants in this investment will receive Series A Preferred Stock.
The Preferred Stock will be entitled to receive cumulative dividends at a rate of 6
percent per year.
35
Copyright 2023, MedFire Innovations, Inc. All Rights Reserved

**Layout**

Title 'Dividend' at top left beside a dotted crosshair guide. MedFire Innovations logo at top right. A large rounded panel fills the centre with a centred blue question, a red highlighted banner line and a three-line centred paragraph. Round page-number badge at bottom right and a copyright footer centred at the bottom.

**Visuals**

MedFire Innovations logo top right: red and blue circular emblem with a white heartbeat/ECG line and a blue wave at the bottom, next to blue 'MedFire' wordmark with an orange-red flame over the 'i', and 'INNOVATIONS' in letter-spaced blue capitals beneath. Large pale red curved arcs (14% opacity) sweep in the top right corner and across the bottom left corner. A dark blue dashed vertical line near the left edge and a red dashed horizontal line under the title form a crosshair. The central panel is light grey with a thick blue outline, large corner radius and a soft drop shadow. A solid red rectangle sits behind the white 'A 6% Yearly Dividend' text. At bottom right is a circular page-number badge with a blue and red swirling ring outline around '35'. A light grey box sits behind the footer text.

**Design**

Background: Solid #F5F5F5 with pale red (#B5121B at 14% opacity) curved arcs in the top right and bottom left corners. Off-white background #F5F5F5 with pale red arc decorations. Title in bold Arial, red #B5121B, about 8% of slide height. Question in bold Arial, royal blue #103595, about 8%. Banner text bold white Arial about 11% on red #B5121B. Body text regular Arial, near-black #181818, about 4%. Panel fill #D9D9D9 with blue #103595 stroke about 0.5% of width, corner radius about 10% of height, soft shadow. Footer is small Arial, black, on a #EEEEEE box. Page number is bold blue.

### Slide 37

**Text**

MedFire Innovations, Inc.
2425 Camino Del Rio S, Suite #125
San Diego, CA 92108 USA
(888)560-1140
medfireinfo@medfire.com
www.medfire.com
Copyright 2022, MedFire Innovations Inc. All rights reserved.

**Layout**

Full-bleed flag photo with a large translucent blue diagonal panel; contact text block left-aligned at about 39% from the left in the vertical middle of the slide; MedFire logo in the bottom-right corner; a copyright line sits at the very bottom, partly hidden by the photo.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a waving American flag viewed from below against a blue sky with white clouds. Stars and red and white stripes are in sharp focus on the left, and the flag is blurred toward the right. A translucent blue (#103595, 86% opacity) parallelogram panel slants from the lower left to the upper right and covers the centre of the slide. Its left edge runs from about x=12% at the bottom to x=38% at the top, and its right edge from about x=70% at the bottom to x=100% at the top. Two thin diagonal stripes run along the left edge of the panel: a narrow white stripe at the bottom left, and a thin blue stripe, with a lighter blue one near the top. The MedFire Innovations logo is at the bottom right: a red circle with a blue and white heart-monitor pulse emblem, then 'MedFire' in blue and red with a small red flame above the 'i', and 'INNOVATIONS' in spaced blue capitals underneath.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of an American flag against a blue sky (base fill #F5F5F5), covered by a translucent blue diagonal panel #103595. Photo background overlaid with a blue panel (#103595 at 86%). All text is white (#FFFFFF) in Montserrat, a geometric sans-serif. The company name is bold at about 5.9% of slide height, and the address lines are regular at about 3.9%. No shadows or rounded corners. The copyright line is small, grey (#A6A6A6) Arial Narrow Bold.
